This was necessary: `.make-settings` assigns `REDIS_CFLAGS` with `=`, so LTO flags placed there were silently erased on any subsequent build.\n- Un-nested the `BUILD_TLS=yes` block so TLS is independent of optimization level.\n\nTwo further issues surfaced during verification, both pre-existing rather than part of the sabotage:\n\n- **A command-line `OPTIMIZATION` was being ignored on an existing build tree.** Persisted `OPT` from `.make-settings` overrode it, so `make OPTIMIZATION=-O3` after a default build recompiled nothing and produced no LTO.
